# BRC-EXP-001 Slice A execution report — stopped on frozen resource limit

## 1. Disposition and authorization boundary

Slice A is **not complete and not offered for acceptance**. This is the separately
authorized execution report at the requested completion-report path, recording a
mandatory stop, not a successful completion certificate.

The accepted synthesis section 10 requires measuring base and applicable BH
worlds and says: “Any valid world excess stops execution and reports the exact
dimension”. The required same-cohort BH world has **12,367 expanded nodes** against
the literal **12,000** ceiling. The executable admission attempt refused with
`input_refused / nodes_exceeded` before experimental lower reconstruction or
ranking. No cap was increased, no proof stripped, and no approved assumption changed.

This is evidence about this constructed valid world, not a proof that every
possible alternative public world must exceed the cap. Selecting an alternative
after this explicit stop, changing the cap, omitting BH, or changing frozen
premises requires owner direction. No architectural milestone is recommended.
Mode B and Slice B remain unauthorized and were not implemented or simulated.

Starting branch: `brc-exp001`.
Starting HEAD/design commit: `53e4c3122bccacef0fb63712c28196584b286664`.
Production baseline and local `origin/main`:
`debcffec5a77ae270bbf8822f82ef1aa7556c5bd`.
The starting tracked tree was clean; the only ordinary untracked files were the
three accepted owner-gate artifacts named below. No fetch, push, merge, branch
creation, reset, rebase, production promotion, or ADR allocation occurred.

The last three files are intentionally retained **untracked and byte-for-byte
unchanged** because the execution stopped without an accepted frozen result.
They were not deleted or committed. The report-only retention endpoint for a
successful Slice A has not been reached; this is explicitly a blocked checkpoint.

Both retained request candidates use canonical compact UTF-8 JSON, sorted keys,
no trailing newline, no NaN/Infinity. Extract these exact keys, serialize by that
same rule, and hash the resulting bytes:

`schema, mode, algorithm, limits, hypotheses, measurements, compatibility,
justifications, scope, policy`.

For both candidates the projection is exactly 29,153 bytes and exactly
`4c5e48452ee81a5c65cef2a51dcae8de56262684e7056be429dd6166788241dc`.
Only `lower` and `lower_commitments` were added. Hypotheses, procedures, outcomes,
predictions, provenance, scope, prior and cost policy were not substituted.

## 3. Public synthetic setup and measured discrepancy

Setup used public accepted record constructors, stores, queries, scripted
ingestion, temporal projection, binding and source interpretation. All setup
stores were temporary and outside the repository. Setup was separate from the
pure experimental validation module and did not consult H/T predictions to
manufacture lower findings. H, prediction and ranking statements were never
appended to any setup ledger.

The base world has 17 records: one logical entity, one variable, two ports,
connection/direction/flow declarations, numeric and semantic declarations,
variable-port and flow-measurement association, and explicit governing criterion.
Observed synthetic scalar Y is 60.1; its governing upper bound is 50. Public FC
is applicable, governing CN is violated, bounded EA is complete, and independently
nominated alignment is attached and content-equal with matched fingerprint.
Cutoff is fixed at 2026-08-07T21:00:00+00:00; receipt at 20:00:06 is 3,594 seconds
earlier in the declared two-hour horizon. No current timestamp was used.

The setup preserves actual accepted Moonraker adapter provenance. Its server
properties are scripted synthetic numeric payloads, not claims about a physical
server, printer, fault, safety condition or actual device. In the BH variant both
scripted server properties carry 60.1. Source quantity/unit and observation-role
interpretations are explicit declarations, not invented producer observations.
Literal adapter/property names in complete lower proof are not relabelled.

The BH size control adds six records: a second variable registration, numeric
specification, quantity and role declarations, behavior registration, and an
exact numeric equality declaration. Both distinct variables have the same exact
semantic lock and observations from the same acquisition cohort. This is the
Slice A observed BH capacity/control preparation required by plan step 5, not
Mode B population-gap derivation.

Public `BehaviorEvaluationQueries` returned:
`execution=executed`, `computed_relation=equal`,
`cohort_admission=same_acquisition_cohort`.
An independent `BehaviorEvaluationResult.model_validate` reconstruction of the
retained BH payload succeeded. Its complete public material revision equals
FC's, its context equals FC's, and its cutoff is the same exact fixed instant.
No detached or stripped BH proof was used.

| Measured request dimension | Base candidate | BH-carrying candidate | Frozen ceiling |
| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| Request bytes | 94819 | 189522 | 524288 |
| Model records | 17 | 23 | 32 |
| Base lower roots | 3 | 3 | 3 |
| Optional BH roots | 0 | 1 | 1 |
| Hypotheses / measurements / outcomes each | 2 / 2 / 2 | 2 / 2 / 2 | 2 / 2 / 3 |
| Compatibility cells | 8 | 8 | 12 |
| Full owned statements | 21 | 21 | 24 |
| Wire nodes | 6058 | 11343 | 12000 |
| Expanded nodes | 7082 | **12367** | **12000** |
| Maximum depth after expansion | 15 | 16 | 48 |

The BH candidate already exceeds the expanded-node ceiling **before adding the
BH-specific hypothesis requirement**. It therefore cannot serve as the required
BH conflict/valid twin in this shape. The primary approved hypothesis bytes were
left intact during this resource probe. No BH comparison was falsely claimed as
a completed Mode A hypothesis-consistency test.

Two independent counts agree:

1. Recursive expansion replaces each closed `statement_ref` object with its
   resolved approved full Statement, then counts object keys, containers and
   scalar values, root depth 1.
2. An iterative stack counts 11,343 wire nodes. There are 16 reference occurrences;
   each replacement adds exactly 64 nodes. Thus
   `11343 + 16 * 64 = 12367`, exceeding 12,000 by **367**.

Repeated lower proof is charged on every serialized occurrence. No deduplication,
node discount, omitted source, changed Statement sharing or narrowed public
schema was used to make the request appear smaller. The provisional Mode A
admission code independently raised `nodes_exceeded` during Statement expansion,
before entering `admit_lower` or creating survivor sets for this candidate.

Other resource dimensions are not declared fully verified. In particular, a
schema-wide generated-trace bound and every exact-limit/plus-one twin were still
unfinished when the mandatory stop fired. The preliminary implementation's
handwritten trace estimate is not accepted as that proof.

## 4. Red-before and provisional primary evidence

Before creating the experimental module, the focused interface tests genuinely
failed on absent `brc_mode_a`: **4 failed, 2 passed**. The four failures were
`ModuleNotFoundError` for the required execute, validate_result, preflight and
admit_lower surface. The two passes were production-path absence and a negative
isolation canary: deliberately adding the experiment path made the dependency
guard assert/fail, as required. This is a surface-level isolation check, not a
complete static/dynamic import audit.

A provisional Mode A implementation was then written only in a temporary root.
Its primary call returned status `preferred_within_supplied_set`, scores T1=1
and T2=2, and recommendation T1. Its provisional result read check returned true.
This was an early development check, **not the completed reconstruction,
adversarial, epistemic-closure and retention validation required for acceptance**.

The observed provisional output was 113,953 bytes, with observed SHA-256
`3507b03c00bc013d340d10f14d26179fafcd7a0c32b432f23eca1d5e187dd03e`.
Those output bytes were not frozen before the stop. This number is development
log evidence only, **not an accepted or retained result commitment**. There is
no RC4-complete canonical result byte bundle and no result artifact to replay.
The implementation was not rerun after the discrepancy to manufacture one.

A fixed-after focused suite was not completed. Four absent-surface red failures
must not be converted into claimed mathematical green counts merely because
function names now exist. Known unfinished work includes exact refusal envelopes,
complete public selector handling, admission-order hardening, exhaustive forged
nested validation, pure-I/O guards, and schema-wide bounds proof.

## 5. Mathematics, cases and unclosed acceptance

The approved primary cells are complete and unchanged:

| Supplied branch | H1 | H2 | Expected survivor set |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| T1/high | permits | excludes | {H1} |
| T1/low | excludes | permits | {H2} |
| T2/code_a | permits | permits | {H1,H2} |
| T2/code_b | permits | permits | {H1,H2} |

Thus, with both baseline checks satisfied, the manual calculation is:
S(T1,O1=low)={H2}; S(T1,O2=high)={H1};
S(T2,O1=code_a)={H1,H2}; S(T2,O2=code_b)={H1,H2}.
The canonical presentation orders T1's actual labels high before low.
Worst(T1)=max(1,1)=1; worst(T2)=max(2,2)=2; primary/final best={T1}.
These are approved expected mathematics, corroborated by the provisional call,
not a substitute for the required independent oracle over an accepted admitted
matrix. That implementation-independent oracle was **not completed**.

## 6. Epistemic, spill and objective disposition

The preserved assumptions remain H at level 3/candidate_hypothesis/assumed;
conditional cells at level 4/predicted_outcome/assumed; procedure, decoder,
conditions and citations retain their exact supplied-assumption classification.
Full lower public observations/evaluated results retain level 1, declarations
level 2. Intended scores are level 5/calculated; preference level 6/bounded
inference; unavailable global completeness level 7/unknown. These are the
accepted schema obligations, not proof that the unfinished result implementation
preserves every closure under attack.

The permitted conclusion remains “preferred within the explicitly supplied
candidate set under `finite_compatibility_minimax/v1`”. H is not cause;
prediction is not observation; compatibility is not physical influence;
within-set preference is not global optimum. No probability or hidden prior,
silent cost preference, actual device/health/safety conclusion, or LLM-proposed
engineering fact was authorized.

The exhaustive executable spill suite was not completed. Incomplete must not
become false; unknown must not become incompatible; assumptions must not become
ledger truth; tied IDs must not silently select a winner; result validation must
not perform filesystem/store/network/device access. These remain unclosed
acceptance conditions, not retrospectively marked passed.

Objectives 3, 4 and 7 receive preliminary design/development evidence only and
are not closed by this stopped execution. Objectives 5 and 6 remain open.
No Gate 1–6 architectural conclusion follows from a request resource failure.
Gate 7, Experiment invalid or inconclusive, is the report-level disposition:
inconclusive execution with no future milestone authorized. No fabricated
completed GateResult object or Mode B outcome is emitted.

## 7. Repository verification and regression

Backend compilation completed successfully using a temporary pycache prefix,
not repository bytecode output. Dependency check reported
`No broken requirements found.`; pip also disclosed its unwritable home-cache
warning and disabled that cache. No installation or dependency change occurred.

Full production regression command:
`PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 .venv/bin/python -m pytest -o addopts='' -p no:cacheprovider tests/ -q`.
It completed with **4073 passed, 1 error in 2066.24s (0:34:26)**, exit 1.
The sole error was setup of
`tests/data_sources/test_http_api_connector.py::TestRealLoopbackTransport::test_get_redirect_refusal_and_proxy_isolation`:
`PermissionError: [Errno 1] Operation not permitted` while creating the localhost
server socket. This is the established sandbox loopback restriction, not a
reported assertion failure.

That exact single test was rerun with separately permitted execution outside the
sandbox, the same Python/pytest flags and unchanged production code: **1 passed
in 7.02s**, exit 0. Accounting is **4073 suite passes + 1 isolated permitted pass
= 4074**, not an assertion that the original sandbox run had zero errors.

The exact original suite log is 96 lines, 6500 bytes, SHA-256
`a765954f04c4e57d1001e7c9e8437c2104e6544d93183e1b62c30b9681ae0c22`.
The following standard-base64 log bundle preserves its original bytes, including
traceback paths and runtime-only metadata; it is not a canonical experiment result.
